let body = "";
let count = 0;

req.on("data", (chunk) => {
    count++;

    console.log("Chunk number:", count);
    console.log("Chunk size:", chunk.length);

    body += chunk;
});

req.on("end", () => {
    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

req.on("end", () => {
    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

    count++;

    console.log("Chunk number:", count);
    console.log("Chunk size:", chunk.length);

    body += chunk;
});

req.on("end", () => {
    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");

    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");

req.on("data", (chunk) => {
    count++;

    console.log("Chunk number:", count);
    console.log("Chunk size:", chunk.length);

    body += chunk;
});

req.on("end", () => {
    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

req.on("end", () => {
    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});

req.on("end", () => {
    console.log("Total chunks:", count);
    console.log("Total data size:", body.length);

    let data = JSON.parse(body);

    console.log("Total users:", data.users.length);

    res.end("Data received successfully");
});
